Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the crystals object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 103. The moduli space has dimension at most 516 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to crystals theory, we decompose the problem into 103 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 516. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
